Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on enzymes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 677. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 817 with relations in degree 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^817(S, F) is trivial. QED.
